Source SHA-256: f4a7ff5bf44d335995ad53319da9b7eb7dc704b67754aedc65a22719b9fa3ece

[Original lines 1-64]
# Purpose Context

Purpose Context constructs reusable indexes over selected governing source and
supplies the constraints needed for an actual purpose. Agents author the meaning;
one deterministic engine verifies bindings, selects rules and renders/checks the
context. An index is comparable to a database covering index: it serves a defined
family of queries while the original source remains authoritative.

Start with the [canonical skill](skills/stdo-context/SKILL.md) and its
[catalog](skills/stdo-context/indexes/CATALOG.json). Through STDO Skills, run the
installed adapter's `provider.py describe` and follow the returned physical skill
path. Direct consumers use `skills/stdo-context/scripts/context.py`. Both paths
use the same engine and independently selected public manifest pin.

The caller's ticket is the durable reference for its work state; the selected
governing sources own the rules and current workspace evidence establishes actual
results. Purpose Context derives the constraints needed for the next decision.
Its output is a derived view, not a replacement ticket or authority. An agent's
context is disposable once required facts and results are retained. Resume through
the current work record and its linked evidence.

| Actual purpose | Current index and source | Usage |
|---|---|---|
| Work and ticket continuity, bounded context, QA, causal triage/re-entry, repair, boundary decisions and evidence reuse | `stdo-development-sdlc-workflow-v1`; 40 rules over 12 source owners in an explicit development candidate | [SDLC workflow](skills/stdo-context/references/sdlc-workflow.md) |
| Pure/local Python 3.12 refactoring under adopted DESIGN_MODULE, including conditional ODD/UX law | `stdo-3.0.5-rc.1-python-pure-refactor-v2`; 113 rules over 16 owners from exact STDO 3.0.5-rc.1 | [Python refactoring](skills/stdo-context/references/python-pure-refactor.md) |

Bind the actual selected source, choose the matching purpose, supply observed
query facts, then select, consume and check the complete received context. Read
the deciding source whenever context is missing, uncertain or insufficient.
A development-source index requires explicit selection/adoption or an admitted
trial alongside the caller's complete operative constitution. Installation does
not make that choice. A different purpose follows the same generic prepare,
author, validate, build and check path when authoring is in scope.

`covered: true` needs an applicable external assessment for the exact index,
corpus and query, with no material unknowns. It concerns the declared governing
constraints; it does not admit work or prove a repair, code result or release.
An authorized trial can use an unassessed context with `covered: false`.
Unknown facts, unsupported purposes and changed sources remain explicit; a new
query receives no inherited verdict. Requirements for independent assessment
come from the governing work contract.

The [SDLC rebuilding guide](skills/stdo-context/references/sdlc-workflow.md#source-led-rebuilding)
connects Product requirements, source closure, authored rules and public bindings.
Affected source, purpose or requirement changes require impact review and
regeneration before reuse. Preserve evidence that still applies to its exact
claim; refresh consumer pins after final public bytes are stable.

[PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json](PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json) pins the portable public files.
The implementation uses Python 3.11+ and the standard library, with no model,
provider or representation runtime dependency. This is unversioned development
source. The construction basis is STDO 3.0.4; release-target indexing follows the
[method-target policy](skills/stdo-context/references/method-targets.md). The latest
selected releasable target is 3.0.5-rc.1; no 3.0.6 cut is claimed.

Historical consumers retain the exact four-owner, 68-rule
[3.0.4 P1 artifact](skills/stdo-context/indexes/stdo-3.0.4-python-pure-refactor-p1/PROVENANCE.json)
and sixteen-owner [3.0.4 successor](skills/stdo-context/indexes/stdo-3.0.4-python-pure-refactor-v2/COVERAGE-STANDING.json).
The separate [3.0.5-rc.1 coverage record](skills/stdo-context/indexes/stdo-3.0.5-rc.1-python-pure-refactor-v2/COVERAGE-STANDING.json)
binds its exact external judgment and evidence reuse limits. Failed predecessors
and original evidence remain intact. None transfers coverage to another query.

Ownership and commitments: [Product](specification/PRODUCT.md),
[requirements](specification/requirements/README.md), [design](design/DESIGN.md).

===== END SOURCE =====

Source SHA-256: f940e0e7e034f99c49eac41783308f0d2b855ace0634ad4b1741fc0d6a895e4b

[Original lines 1-30]
# Purpose Context intent

Make the governing context for an actual purpose explicit, reusable and
inspectable. A purpose context index is analogous to a database covering index:
it materializes the meanings needed by a family of queries over a selected
source corpus. It saves repeated construction without making the source
dispensable or turning a mechanical validity check into semantic assurance.

The owner selected direct source → purpose context index on 2026-11-12 and
assigned it a separate Product. Representation work serves documentation and
visualisation. The shared engine already demonstrated in the bounded comparison
is extracted intact; this is not a competing reimplementation.

The immediate use is code constraints for pure/local Python refactoring.
Broader build and SDLC purposes require their own selected sources, declared
facts, authored index and evidence. Utility is tested through actual tasks;
logical or schema consistency alone cannot establish the value hypothesis.

The admitted SDLC extension now includes ordinary QA, causal triage and owner
re-entry, bounded repair/rechecks, boundary decisions and evidence reuse. Its
separate purpose and rebuild requirements preserve the older Python purposes;
new workflow constraints are authored from owning source and assessed on their
own exact subjects.

The continuation correction makes the existing workflow purpose explicit:
work survives an actor's disposable context through its selected ticket or
other lawful carrier, current workspace and recorded results. The index must
retain the governing duties for constructing context, preserving facts and
reconciling work on restart. Actual use, including stale accounts and uncertain
effects, decides the bounded capability claim.

===== END SOURCE =====

Source SHA-256: cdee9c4d84b235914f6fedb8f1bd7a56a5d62a0557dac5c701fd7aceec5b1671

# Purpose Context Product

## Product boundary

Purpose Context owns the purpose context index contract, canonical authoring
and consumption instructions, source paging/search, deterministic validation,
rule selection, Markdown rendering, artifact checks and reusable index catalog.
Its stable definition ID is `urn:stdo:product-definition:purpose-context`.

The runtime accepts an explicit source corpus, exact method identity, purpose
and query facts. It returns selected rules with conditions, exceptions,
dependencies, precedence, source grounds and unresolved decisions preserved.
A catalog index must match the caller's actual source membership/order/bytes,
method, purpose and family applicability. New source or purpose requires re-entry.

The corpus identity names the reusable source, not a downstream project.
Target Product, task and existing grant are caller coordinates. Multiple projects
may reuse the same authored index when the governing inputs actually match.

## Construction authority

The owner selected this Product and the extraction in the
[alignment activation](../.ai-workspace/work/ALIGNMENT_2026-11-12/ACTIVATION.json).
The Product Definition pins installed STDO 3.0.4 and the
[reference-frame basis](REFERENCE_FRAME_BASIS.md). This constructor basis is
separate from each caller's source/method selection. No all-3.* compatibility
is implied by the initial catalog entry.

## Relations and exclusions

STDO Skills owns host adapters, discovery and exact dependency pins. It reads
this Product's one canonical skill and invokes its CLI. It does not own a
second engine. Specification Methodology owns the governing STDO sources and
native method toolchain. Representation/Axiom may support visual documentation;
neither is a Purpose Context runtime dependency.

This Product grants no task authority, solves no task, proves no code correct
and makes no release decision. Mechanical validation and content equality do
not prove semantic completeness or that a recipient actually received a body.
Assessment and native delivery evidence remain separately bound subjects.

## Constraint coverage and task verification

A purpose declares the predicate its context is sufficient to decide. The
`python-pure-refactor/2` successor evaluates retention of applicable governing
method constraints before construction. A complete conditional obligation to
establish an actual domain, authority premise, design relation or proof does
not assert that the task already established it. The consumer must acquire its
concrete Product inputs and perform those obligations before the dependent
action or claim. Missing governing meaning or unknown requested permission
premises remain context gaps; future unperformed verification has no result.

The successor retains conditional ODD/UX branches within the original family,
uses explicit requested-permission predicates and never makes false profile
facts a family prerequisite. The complete source selection, purpose and exact
query bind each external semantic assessment. Product-specific and separately
adopted law beyond that scope remains a declared authoritative task input.
Finite source assessment is externally challengeable, not universal completeness.

## Current standing

Current source is an unversioned development Product. The concrete initial
index is the selected P1 working basis, ported by envelope rebinding while
preserving all 68 rule objects, processing ledger and unresolved entries.
Its historical 12-source-discriminator audit and three checked task outcomes
are finite observations, not new subject coverage. New contexts stay
`covered: false` without an exact separately selected assessment; material
unknowns still block coverage. An existing authorized experimental use may
carry a trial binding without creating new approvals or self-certifying.

The coverage successor retains all 68 original rule lineages and adds 45
source-derived imported/profile rules over sixteen exact installed owners.
Its 51 gap dispositions distinguish imported law, actual applicability or
permission premises, and task parameters/future verification. The
[current coverage record](../skills/stdo-context/indexes/stdo-3.0.4-python-pure-refactor-v2/COVERAGE-STANDING.json)
binds the separately supplied supported assessment for exact candidate03/Q0
and the retained falsified candidate02. The unchanged authoring provenance is
historical. A new query receives no inherited verdict; this catalog or its
author never self-certifies it. Original P1 remains exact and its historical
evidence is not transferred.

Public qualification, publication and general effectiveness remain open.

## Latest releasable index target

The owner selected the latest releasable semantic source for release-target
index authoring, independently of this Product's older construction basis.
The separate `stdo-3.0.5-rc.1-python-pure-refactor-v2` entry binds the latest
selected published native cut and the same sixteen-owner closure. Its 113
rule meanings and family are conserved, with two RELEASE source coordinates
adjusted against exact matching cited text. This is an authored conservation
claim pending independent review; the 3.0.4 assessment is not transferred.

The [method-target policy](../skills/stdo-context/references/method-targets.md)
requires reconciling distributed indexes to the final cut at packaging while
preserving evidence whose actual source/consumer applicability is established.
Older consumers continue to use their selected exact basis and matching index.
The retained 3.0.4 P1/v2 assets and evidence stay unchanged. This source work
declares no 3.0.6 cut or implicit consumer migration.

The [latest-release coverage record](../skills/stdo-context/indexes/stdo-3.0.5-rc.1-python-pure-refactor-v2/COVERAGE-STANDING.json) binds the admitted external
assessment for the exact 3.0.5-rc.1 index/corpus/new Q0 and retains its
prior-evidence reuse limits and residuals. Author-stage candidate or pending
wording above and in frozen provenance is historical; this separate record
supplies current standing. New queries inherit no verdict, and task success
and final release qualification remain separate.

## SDLC workflow context

The owner admitted the separate `sdlc-workflow/1` purpose on 2026-11-14. Its
[requirements](requirements/SDLC-WORKFLOW.md) govern ordinary QA, causal
triage and owner re-entry, bounded repair/rechecks, named boundary decisions,
drift control and valid evidence reuse. The same workflow purpose retains
ticket/carrier continuity, bounded context construction, durable step-result
recording and resumption from current work state without private conversation
history. Its source-owned conditions do not require new facts or a new carrier.
Source authoring begins at those
requirements and the full applicable governing source closure; the generated
index is a derived view, never the requirement or method authority.

A frozen development-source manifest identifies the selected changed defaults
and their unchanged source owners. Its exact published predecessor is native
`v3.0.5-rc.1`, manifest
`64c026667abba933d289317793dbe80b5f9ab822a9870307795584402e460e1c`.
The candidate is explicitly unversioned development source. The constructor
remains installed 3.0.4, older indexes remain exact, and callers must explicitly
select/adopt the development projection before reuse. No 3.0.6, publication,
whole-method qualification or migration is implied.

The Product owns the rebuild and invalidation contract. It retains source and
purpose membership/order, authoring grounds/conditions/unknowns, reproducible
generation inputs, old-subject evidence and claim-specific reuse arguments.
An external semantic judgment remains separately supplied for its exact
index/corpus/query. Public manifest regeneration follows all final public
files; it never upgrades the standing of an authored candidate.

# Purpose Context reference-frame basis

This Product uses the exact installed STDO 3.0.4 constitution selected in
`stdo_purpose_context.json`, including its bootstrap, SPEC_METHOD,
REFERENCE_FRAME and EXECUTIVE_BASELINE entrypoints as applicable.
The owner selected Product repricing and extraction on 2026-11-12. Root binds
the cross-Product outcome, and a separate Writer performs the authorized
Purpose Context source effects under the retained alignment activation.

The accepted implementation boundary is the [minimal extraction design](../design/DESIGN.md):
one existing deterministic engine plus portable catalog binding and reuse.
Unknown source/purpose applicability is not silently closed. Independent
assessment and release requirements apply when selected by the governing
contract; local deterministic checks do not supply that standing.

This basis introduces no local axioms or overrides. The initial catalog's
adopted-DESIGN_MODULE fact concerns its consumer workload; it is not blanket
qualification of the extracted runtime against every possible design profile.

## Adopted SDLC workflow guidance

The owner admission recorded in `SDLC_DEFAULTS_2026-11-14`, continued by
`TICKET_CONTEXT_CONSTITUTION_2026-11-14`, selects the
[default SDLC projection](../skills/stdo-context/indexes/stdo-development-sdlc-workflow-v1/source/standards/SDLC_DEFAULTS.md)
for Purpose Context work, SHA-256
`a04335fe75f38c665055c8272f6707fe9e244b7fece5bed936332d0c82e1aac8`.
Its original source is the Method Product's
`build_tenants/common/standards/SDLC_DEFAULTS.md`. The complete governing
constitution remains installed 3.0.4 at the pin above; the projection creates
no partial constitution. Nine selected non-release owning members match
that install byte-for-byte. TICKETS adds the narrow carrier-to-context and
continuation application of its existing authority/continuity law and installed
ACTIVATION §2/5/7; it does not replace those owners or create a new carrier.
The selected RELEASE acceptance/successor sections
also match byte-for-byte although their whole owner changed in the later cut.
The development index retains the later target identity separately.

The default work path binds current subject and intended outcome, performs
incremental QA, diagnoses a discrepancy before choosing repair, re-enters the
supported cause's owner, verifies the bounded result and returns for lawful
continuation or closure. Existing gates and unknowns remain. A recurring
failure or overloaded context triggers the reference-frame diagnostic order;
it does not authorize repeated patches or upstream meaning changes.

Ticketed work starts from its current admitted record and linked governing
inputs, results and evidence, with the current workspace deciding actual state.
Use another sufficient carrier where the selected law permits it. Construct
bounded context with qualified resolvable references; measure before reading
where the activation declares a context capacity, retaining unmeasured host
context outside any fit claim. The granted Writer records needed step facts,
results, residuals, owner rulings and continuation before return. Resumption
checks current state and invalidators and reconciles uncertain effects before
repetition or dependent work. A stale summary supplies no competing authority;
discarding private context resets nothing and adds no approval requirement.

The current cross-Product activation selects coordinated Executive, separate
Writers and source-first Reviewer activations under the installed
EXECUTIVE_BASELINE. An ordinary sufficient direct Writer remains available
under the accepted configuration. The activation supplies exact territory,
capability/context/time envelope, evidence carrier and stopping condition;
this basis supplies no permanent actor or universal review round count.

This Product keeps technical severity, priority, boundary effect and
disposition separate. Applicable non-waivable hard stops, exact claim failure,
authority conflicts and missing required evidence/independence withhold their
dependent boundary. Other findings are ranked for the actual outcome by
consequence, likelihood/exposure, reversibility, dependencies and repair cost;
an adjacent finding returns to its owner without widening this work.
No numeric cutoff or review ratio is adopted here. Any decision requiring a
more specific scale/cutoff acquires it from the owning work policy first.

Repair checks are scoped by actual impact, with failing-before/passing-after
reproduction and the declared suite when reproducible. Required independent
rechecks follow the selected baseline's four conditions. Tests do not replace
required assessment. Retain demonstrably applicable support, invalidate affected
results before reliance, and reacquire owners when a source/projection/frame
binding changes. Mandatory gates remain even when evidence is reused.

The original evidence remains in this Product's
`.ai-workspace/work/SDLC_DEFAULTS_2026-11-14/`. The continuation uses the root
cross-Product `.ai-workspace/work/TICKET_CONTEXT_CONSTITUTION_2026-11-14/`
record, with this Writer's return under `index-writer/`; its activation supplies
coordination and independent-review boundaries.
Future work binds its own admitted carrier and exact identities. Context
coverage is a constraint claim; Product success, native receipt and any release
acceptance remain separately evaluated claims.

# Purpose Context requirements

| ID | Observable obligation | Deciding negative case |
|---|---|---|
| PC-R01 | Public discovery checks the caller-selected manifest and every declared public member. | Changed catalog, index, schema or runtime bytes refuse under the old pin. |
| PC-R02 | Bind the actual installed method manifest and ordered selected source files; disclose the check scope. | Expected digest text with changed/missing actual manifest or source refuses. |
| PC-R03 | Reuse only exact catalog source, purpose and family matches. | Wrong purpose/method/member order/false family refuses; missing or ill-typed family facts return incomplete. |
| PC-R04 | Reuse an unchanged authored index for independent queries and target projects. | Changing target coordinates cannot rebind corpus identity or trigger authoring. |
| PC-R05 | Preserve authored deciding meaning and exact predecessor identity. | The original P1 port stays byte-identical; a separately named semantic successor individually accounts for changed conditions, permissions, dependencies, grounds and uncertainty without rewriting the predecessor. |
| PC-R06 | Distinguish mechanical validity, assessed coverage and delivered identity. | Self-assessment, empty evidence or material unknown cannot produce a qualified covered claim. |
| PC-R07 | Enforce the caller's whole-context UTF-8 byte budget without truncation. | An insufficient bound produces no partial context directory. |
| PC-R08 | Permit explicit new corpora/purposes through the same preparation, authoring and compilation path. | A non-Python documentation purpose cannot require a representation runtime or Python workload fact. |
| PC-R09 | Keep portable source/index assets independent of machine paths and host aliases. | Materialized public copies and both host symlink routes resolve the same checked runtime. |
| PC-R10 | Preserve experiment boundaries. | Historical P1 task outcomes or processing ledgers cannot certify a new source/purpose/query or all STDO releases. |

The canonical authoring contract and index schema are public runtime resources.
Source-semantic adequacy and task-value hypotheses require their own finite
tests; these requirements do not replace them with checksum or schema checks.

The coverage successor adds these bounded requirements:

| ID | Observable obligation | Deciding negative case |
|---|---|---|
| PC-R11 | Distinguish complete preconstruction method constraints from concrete task admission, verification and acceptance. | A future strict pass or no-delta proof must not be fabricated as a query fact or inferred from context coverage. |
| PC-R12 | Retain the original family's conditional ODD/UX cases. | A catalog filter requiring both profiles false, or missing scope treated as false, cannot complete the family. |
| PC-R13 | A requested mutation, failure-carrier departure or typing escape retains its exact source premise conjunction. | Unknown requested evidence withholds coverage; a false request grants no exception and cannot conceal an already planned departure. |
| PC-R14 | Account for all 51 predecessor gaps with source-backed dispositions and separately assessed exact query bindings. | Merely clearing unknowns, author self-review or an assessment of another query cannot establish supported coverage. |

The selected coverage predicate is finite and source-relative. An index does
not certify every Product-specific relation or absent future implementation;
those task inputs and results remain explicitly required at their own boundary.

The release-target successor adds these selection and conservation obligations:

| ID | Observable obligation | Deciding negative case |
|---|---|---|
| PC-R15 | Author a release-target index against the explicitly selected latest releasable semantic source, independently of the Product construction basis; consume only a caller's exact selected basis. | An older constructor pin cannot silently select stale target law; a newer catalog entry cannot migrate an older consumer. |
| PC-R16 | At packaging, reconcile source closure and index bindings to the actual final native cut and preserve only demonstrably applicable prior evidence. | A future version label, stale source/member pin or an assessment bound to a predecessor index/corpus is refused as support for the new subject. |

The [method-target policy](../../skills/stdo-context/references/method-targets.md)
keeps authoring, consumption and final packaging selections distinct. These
requirements do not create a new release gate for unrelated work or mandate
rerunning unaffected evidence merely because a version string changed.

The [SDLC workflow requirements](SDLC-WORKFLOW.md) govern the separate reusable
workflow purpose, source closure, reproducible rebuilding, invalidation and
claim-specific evidence reuse. They are requirements on this Product, not a
new runtime engine or blanket acceptance policy for consumers.

===== END SOURCE =====

Source SHA-256: 5afba547f7e91fafe4af9c4b475ef936ee94ac1a6587465be4fe4e5419772cf7

[Original lines 1-58]
# SDLC workflow purpose and rebuilding

These requirements are admitted by the 2026-11-14 cross-Product activation.
They extend the Product's reusable context capability; they do not change its
installed 3.0.4 construction constitution or migrate an existing consumer.

| ID | Observable obligation | Deciding negative case |
|---|---|---|
| PC-S01 | Supply a separately named SDLC workflow purpose over explicit governing source closure, with actual reusable catalog, purpose, source and index assets. | Relabeling a Python refactor entry, a prose promise, or an index with missing applicable imported law is insufficient. |
| PC-S02 | Retain ordinary work/QA, causal diagnosis and lawful owner re-entry, bounded repair verification and conditional rechecks, continuation/stopping, and named acceptance/release boundaries. | Repeated failure cannot force automatic code repair or specification change; incremental QA cannot imply acceptance or publication. |
| PC-S03 | Preserve exact claims, populations, results and unknowns, and distinguish severity, priority, boundary effect and disposition. | An unperformed predicate receives no result; an adjacent finding cannot silently widen the reviewed subject or block it on severity alone. |
| PC-S04 | Retain applicable mandatory gates and Product-owned policy choices. | A size budget, review count, green suite or default projection cannot waive a hard stop, required independence or missing operation grant. |
| PC-S05 | Declare actual query facts and conditional branches with their evidence meanings. | Missing, null or ill-typed deciding facts remain unknown; fixtures cannot be copied as claims about a live task. |
| PC-S06 | Make source-to-index rebuilding reproducible: bind requirements, selected source membership/order/bytes, purpose/facts, authoring recipe and generator, then validate through the existing deterministic core. | A changed source/recipe/purpose cannot retain old generated bindings by updating a label or digest alone. |
| PC-S07 | Fail closed on changed selected source, source manifest, closure, authoring recipe, purpose, query or public bundle before dependent reliance. | Stale default projection, imported owner or candidate manifest cannot pass an old catalog binding. |
| PC-S08 | Bind an unreleased method candidate as development source with exact predecessor identity and explicit caller selection. | No fabricated release URI/cut, release qualification claim, automatic latest selection or silent consumer migration is allowed. |
| PC-S09 | Preserve all earlier Python 3.0.4/3.0.5 index and evidence bytes; identify impacted claims and reuse only demonstrably applicable evidence. | Unchanged owner meaning is not enough to replay an assessment whose exact index/corpus/query binding changed. |
| PC-S10 | Provide finite positive/negative controls for all binding boundaries, unknown applicability, conditional selection, full-body budgets and retained old paths, plus realistic source-first external assessment subjects. | Checksums, source ledgers and author self-review cannot establish semantic adequacy or actual native task behaviour. |
| PC-S11 | Retain ticket/carrier-based work continuity within the workflow purpose: bind the selected durable work record and current workspace, construct bounded context from qualified governing references, record step-produced facts/results and continuation state before return, and reconstruct after context loss while checking invalidators and reconciling uncertain effects before dependent work. | A chat summary cannot overrule the ticket or current evidence; missing private context cannot reset work, justify blind repetition or conceal an unrecorded result. The requirement creates no extra carrier, universal capacity gate, new approval or independent review beyond the selected law and work grant. |
| PC-S12 | Establish the continuity capability through finite actual-use observations from the current ticket and linked workspace without the prior conversation, with separately derived source-first assessment expectations. | A successful index build, rendered-body check, count of rules, or author-written expected response cannot establish that a fresh actor continued correctly, handled stale evidence or preserved an uncertain effect. |

The coverage predicate is retention of the selected method's applicable SDLC
workflow constraints before the requested decision. It does not assert that a
task is admitted, evidence exists, a fix works, review was independent, a policy
has been selected, or any boundary has passed. Complete conditional obligations
name the evidence and owning Product inputs the consumer must obtain. Unknown
facts deciding which constraints apply remain material gaps.

The continuation capability is part of ordinary work, QA and evidence reuse
in the existing `sdlc-workflow/1` family. Its source-owned conditions stay in
the rules; additional boolean facts must not make retaining results, observing
current evidence, or lawful restart optional. The existing purpose and fact
meanings are preserved unless an explicit semantic change establishes a new
purpose boundary.

Finite actual-use evidence for PC-S11 and PC-S12 includes these situations:

| Situation | Required observation and claim limit |
|---|---|
| A fresh actor receives an admitted ticket and its current linked work state, with no previous conversation. | It recovers applicable progress, governing inputs and the next authorized action, produces the bounded result, and retains required facts/evidence before return. No mandatory transcript or new carrier is inferred. |
| A retained summary contradicts the current ticket or workspace evidence. | Current authoritative state corrects the account; unresolved conflict stays explicit and contradictory acceptance evidence withholds its dependent closure. |
| Material evidence changes while another recorded result remains applicable. | The actor checks invalidators, reopens affected support and preserves independently surviving support with its original subject and evidence. |
| An interrupted effect may have occurred or may still be acting. | The actor reconciles sufficiently before repetition or dependent action, retaining `completion_unknown` or the unresolved end beside `not_effected`; missing history is not evidence of no effect. |
| The selected activation declares a context capacity. | The actor records the selected material's size, unit, boundary, capacity and comparison before reading it; unmeasured host context remains outside any measured fit claim. Insufficient fit follows lawful refinement, sequencing, compression, reassignment or refusal without omitting material obligations. |

These are requirement scenarios, not supplied evaluator answers or claims of
successful performance. An assessor derives its own discriminators from the
owning source before candidate exposure. Record exact task, source, query,
index, received context and actual effects for every performed observation;
keep unperformed cases and broader effectiveness or release claims open.

Rebuild on an affected requirement, selected method/source closure, purpose,
fact meaning, authored rule/ground/condition, generator or catalog contract
change. Compare old and new exact subjects and state the impact. Mechanical
rebuilding may conserve unchanged rule meaning; changed meaning needs source
re-entry. A new external assessment can cite applicable prior evidence but
must bind its own exact subject. Final release packaging reconciles to the
actual immutable cut; development-source identity is not a final release pin.

===== END SOURCE =====

Select [stdo_skills.json](stdo_skills.json), verify its exact installed STDO basis,
then read README, specification/GOALS.md, specification/INTENT.md,
specification/PRODUCT.md, requirements and specification/DESIGN.md. This Product
owns host workflow bindings and installation. Purpose Context owns indexing and
context construction. Representation Tools owns the optional visual runtime.

Current delivery is an unversioned development package. Immutable release records
and previous work receipts retain their original identities and claims. They do
not qualify these changed source bytes or authorize changes to installed bundles.

The native workflow owns intake, effects, assessment, recovery and handoff. Use
existing owner/grant authority within its scope; a skill, route or tool result adds
no authority. Root coordinates cross-Product work; a separately bound Writer
performs exact scoped effects. Source-first independent reviewers derive their
expectations before consuming candidate-produced indexes or results and do not
repair their assessed subject. Preserve unrelated work and historical evidence.

For relevant task context, use skills/stdo-context/SKILL.md to resolve the pinned

Source SHA-256: c799a02c99c5eb24c6e71566788d93fb019e0ff826aafc66aa8e71676bff7de7

[Original lines 1-63]
# STDO Skills

STDO Skills binds the selected native STDO workflows to Codex and Claude. It
uses the independent Purpose Context Product to supply governing constraints for
the actual task. The [thin adapter](skills/stdo-context/SKILL.md) verifies that
Product and follows its canonical skill; Skills owns no second index engine.

Start with [Getting Started](GETTING_STARTED.md) for the first task and expected
outcomes, or [installation](scripts/INSTALLATION.md) for exact setup, verification
and removal commands. Both host bindings use the same installer and context
runtime. Native workflows continue to own work, grants, assessment and handoff.

The ticket remains the durable source of truth for its unit of work. Skills
construct the next task's context from that record and its linked authority and
evidence, then return results through the authorized work-state update. Agent
context and summaries are disposable accounts. [Ticket and context continuity](GETTING_STARTED.md#keep-the-ticket-as-the-work-reference)
explains what to retain and how to resume without reconstructing the conversation.
These obligations come from the selected native TICKETS and ACTIVATION owners,
are carried by the default reference frames and workflow index, and are applied
by the native Skills and adapter. The guides explain that path; they are not its
constitutional authority. [Requirements SK-19/20](specification/requirements/README.md)
bind the consuming behavior and its continuation scenarios.

| Task | Entry path | Expected result |
|---|---|---|
| Deliver admitted work or a repair | `stdo-work`, with workflow context where relevant | Bounded change, appropriate verification and an explicit continuation or closure decision |
| Assess an exact claim | `stdo-review`, with workflow context after any required source-first derivation | Evidence and bounded findings returned to the decision owner; no self-assigned repair or release |
| Resolve entry, record work or inspect progress | `stdo-help`, `stdo-ticket`, `stdo-status` | A lawful next step, durable work carrier or evidence-backed status respectively |
| Acquire task constraints | `stdo-context` | Exact source/purpose/query binding, received context, gaps and assessment standing |

Two current purposes serve different decisions:

- `sdlc-workflow/1`: work → QA → causal triage → owning-layer re-entry →
  verification → continue/close. The 40-rule index is an explicitly selected
  development projection; installation alone does not adopt it. It distinguishes
  incremental QA from named boundary decisions and preserves valid evidence.
- `python-pure-refactor/2`: preconstruction method constraints for pure/local
  Python 3.12 refactoring. The latest release-target entry has 113 rules over
  sixteen owners from exact STDO 3.0.5-rc.1. Older exact entries remain available.

Use the catalog returned by `provider.py describe` to select the actual index,
source and purpose. A purpose name alone does not select a release. New queries
inherit no semantic assessment; unknown facts stay unknown. Governing-context
coverage is separate from code correctness, repair verification and acceptance.

Read [Intent](specification/INTENT.md), [Product](specification/PRODUCT.md),
[requirements](specification/requirements/README.md) and [design](specification/DESIGN.md)
for ownership and commitments. The [Product Definition](stdo_skills.json) selects
STDO 3.0.4 as the construction basis, independently of the target consumer and
indexed source.

Current source builds an **unversioned development package**. Its inventory and
dependency pins are separate from immutable 3.0.5 inventories; no 3.0.6 release
is declared. The earlier nine-case SDLC trial retains its original source and
query subjects; the constitutional continuity increment requires its own exact
index assessment and continuation observations. Automatic host pickup, broader
effectiveness and release qualification remain separate evidence claims.

Context-only installs need Purpose Context and the selected native method.
Optional Representation Tools adds visual documentation through `stdo-project`.
Lean, registry/formal support and the former Skills-owned context implementation
are retired from current source. Historical releases and installed bundles retain
their original bytes and evidence; this package does not migrate them.

===== END SOURCE =====

| GS-02 Reuse Purpose Context indexes | Thin adapter, independent pinned Product and existing-index selection; source-assessed v2 supports exact-query consumption | Actual installed bind/select/consume/check plus mismatch/stale/unknown controls |

| GS-04 Retire obsolete active routes | Old local engine, registry/formal guides and legacy tenant source archived | Exact preimages, coherent current routes and dependency sweep |
| GS-05 Package honestly | Unversioned development inventory; historical releases untouched | Fresh disposable package/install/verify/remove and preserved release hashes |
| GS-06 Deliver the adopted SDLC default | Relevant work/review/refresh routes select the declared workflow purpose and source default | Bounded actual-use evidence for drift, causal re-entry, repair stopping, real boundary assessment, uncertainty and evidence reuse |

[Purpose Context alignment](../../.ai-workspace/work/PURPOSE_CONTEXT_ALIGNMENT_2026-11-12/skills-writer/PLAN.md).

adds an independently judged governing-context subject and fresh local integration.

Make the selected STDO method usable through ordinary Codex and Claude work while
preserving the method's ownership, exact source basis and effect boundaries.

then inspect original sources when required. They should not rebuild the same
context mechanism in every host skill.

The owner selected Purpose Context as an independent Product on 2026-11-12.
STDO Skills consumes its public runtime and index assets. Representation Tools
and STDO Representation serve visual documentation; registry/context/formal
support routes are withdrawn from current Skills scope. This is a source scope
and implementation change, not retroactive satisfaction of old requirements or
a migration of existing installations.

Source SHA-256: e5551b326ccb6906011fdcc124f0486669d3b049938b9042dd6767c8c288ece6

[Original lines 1-95]
# STDO Skills

## Product boundary

Identity: `urn:stdo:product-definition:stdo-skills`.
Bounded context: `urn:stdo-skills:bounded-context:agent-method-application`.

This Product supplies host bindings that help Codex and Claude apply an exactly
selected STDO method. The native core workflows own intake, grants, work, review,
continuation, status and recovery. Skills preserves their canonical instructions
and references instead of creating another lifecycle or mutable authority store.

The independent Purpose Context Product owns source binding, reusable indexes,
selection, context compilation, freshness and its canonical authoring skill.
Skills owns only the adapter that verifies the selected dependency and forwards
its public operations. Both host tenants consume that same Product. The explicit
[composition](composition/PRODUCTS.md#purpose-context) binds the relationship;
sharing a checkout alone does not establish it.

Representation Tools is a separately selected optional component for visual
source/project/code exploration. Context use has no visual, registry or Lean
prerequisite. The old Skills-owned engine, registry guides and formal support
routes are retired; earlier implementations survive only in exact history.

## Selected methodology

[stdo_skills.json](../stdo_skills.json) selects `stdo://releases/v3.0.4/`, manifest
`102817a3f4bea4dca1d6fa0fc634b94404dd2ba82ba6d8c38b273402dda9f95b`.
Resolve and verify that installed cut. The target project's independently selected
method and work grant remain separate from this construction basis. A dependency
or host installation does not adopt a method on a target project's behalf.
Release-targeted indexing follows the latest owner-selected releasable method,
currently exact 3.0.5-rc.1. Its source manifest and index identity remain explicit;
the constructor's older basis is not an index-selection default. Older consumers
continue to require their own exact-basis matching catalog entry. Final packaging
must reconcile the index and assessment with the actual final semantic source
and native cut; it cannot relabel the current evidence as a future release.

## Capabilities and boundaries

Both hosts expose the canonical native core five workflows, with the native
Claude refresh workflow where supplied. At relevant constraint acquisition,
code-build, review, handoff and refresh points they use reusable purpose indexes.
When an index does not match the exact source/method/purpose, the dependency
refuses reuse; authoring a new index is an explicitly scoped follow-on, not an
automatic substitution. Unknown facts and unaudited semantic coverage remain open.

Source-first review derives obligations independently before judging candidate
indexes or implementations. A matching hash, compiled context or passing tool
check does not establish source completeness, semantic correctness or authority.

The default adopted frame basis provides the ordinary SDLC cycle: bounded work,
QA, causal triage, re-entry at the owning layer, verification and a decision to
continue or close. Relevant work, review and refresh routes acquire the declared
SDLC-purpose context. They preserve the current outcome, subject, basis, scope
and decision boundary; a new observation cannot silently expand that contract.
An exact repair is verified by the evidence its claim requires. Unchanged valid
support is reused, and additional independent assessment is conditional on the
selected basis. Required acceptance and release gates remain effective.

The source-backed default is shared method guidance selected by this Product's
frame basis. Consumers bind Product-specific policy choices without having to
recreate shared triage or stopping rules in a local constitution. Skills must
carry these defaults through the actual consumer path and demonstrate bounded
behaviour on the declared scenarios, not merely contain the right words.

The installer creates fresh disposable or explicitly selected workspace bindings
with exact package, method, interpreter and component identities. It verifies and
removes only its receipt-owned roots. It does not mutate global skill directories,
upgrade existing bundles, or rewrite immutable releases.

## Construction authority

Jim owns Product scope, acceptance and release. The 2026-11-12 instruction selects
an independent Purpose Context Product, Skills consumption, visual-only
Representation scopes and removal of obsolete active routes. Root coordinates the
bounded alignment; separately granted Writers own their exact source territories.
The [current frame basis](REFERENCE_FRAME_BASIS.md) preserves direct Writer work
where sufficient and independent review where required. Prior receipts retain
their historical basis and limits; they supply no blanket approval of this source.
The owner's 2026-11-14 instruction additionally admits the shared SDLC default,
Purpose Context coverage and rebuild requirements, and this Product's consuming
bindings under the [bounded work contract](../../.ai-workspace/work/SDLC_DEFAULTS_2026-11-14/ACTIVATION.md).

## Acceptance and release boundary

The current outcome is a coherent, reviewable development source package with
finite disposable installation, dependency and public-path evidence. Required
[scenarios](requirements/SCENARIOS.md) remain separately observable. Mechanical
checks, explicit agent use, automatic host pickup and full native workflow
qualification are separate claims. Unperformed scenarios stay unestablished.

The immutable historical five-Product 3.0.5 contract is not the current six-Product
development inventory. No new version, public release, installed migration or
successor qualification is implied by this source alignment.

===== END SOURCE =====

# Project reference-frame basis

Identity: `urn:stdo-skills:frame-basis:sdlc-defaults:1`.
Construction basis: exact STDO 3.0.4 selected by [Product Definition](../stdo_skills.json).
Scope: the owner's 2026-11-12 Product alignment and 2026-11-13 governing-context
coverage completion and latest-method index correction, plus the approved
2026-11-14 SDLC default and workflow-context delivery. The indexed corpus is selected separately from this
Product's unchanged construction basis.

| Frame | Attention and responsibility | Return and boundary |
|---|---|---|
| Executive | Exact Product outcome, dependency frontier, frozen subjects and existing authority | Coordinates and consumes closed results; does not perform Writer effects |
| Writer / Worker | Exact source territory, preimages, contracts, actual operations and checks | Constructs within grant; retains failed/unperformed checks and residuals |
| Independent reviewer | Source-derived obligations, exact closed candidate and actual-path evidence | Assesses without repair or self-assessment; reports limits and invalidators |
| Direct Writer | One sufficient local outcome with existing basis and effect grant | No mandatory orchestration invented merely because a task exists |

Each activation binds outcome, source and subject identities, territory, authority,
material invariants, tools, consumer and stop/re-entry conditions. Preserve unknown
applicability and original-source access. An index or handoff routes attention;
it cannot replace the deciding source or grant mutation/acceptance authority.

Current evidence must identify the exact package and dependencies, host path,
performed/unperformed predicates and changed subjects. Historical T-001/T-003,
composition and 3.0.5 records remain on their original bases. Their completion or
admission does not qualify these changed source bytes. Product acceptance and
release remain separate from construction and bounded local checks.

## Selected SDLC default

The [owner-admitted work](../../.ai-workspace/work/SDLC_DEFAULTS_2026-11-14/ACTIVATION.md)
selects the shared [SDLC default projection](../../specification_methodology/v3/build_tenants/common/standards/SDLC_DEFAULTS.md#selected-default-flow)
for this Product. Selected projection SHA-256:
`a04335fe75f38c665055c8272f6707fe9e244b7fece5bed936332d0c82e1aac8`.
Its existing-law owners are resolved against the verified installed 3.0.4
constitution. The projection is guidance, not a second constitution. A material
change needs refreshed identity and adoption applicability before reliance.

The default applies bounded work, QA, causal triage, owner re-entry and verification
to the exact claim. Use the sufficient direct Writer path above and coordinate
material boundaries. Reviewer severity informs the decision owner; findings do
not themselves enlarge the grant, assign priority or create another review gate.
The adopted Executive configuration retains its selected repair/recheck rules,
separate landing/closure/release conditions, resource limits and valid-evidence
reuse. Product-specific thresholds and explicit gates remain declared choices.

For workflow constraints, Skills uses the separately selected SDLC purpose index
and original-source routes. A development-source binding does not replace this
installed constitution. Unknown applicability and missing required evidence
withhold their dependent claims while preserving unrelated authorized progress.

The owner's [four-layer continuation](../../.ai-workspace/work/TICKET_CONTEXT_CONSTITUTION_2026-11-14/ACTIVATION.md)
selects this exact corrected projection. TICKETS applies constitutional context
and continuation law through the sufficient carrier; ACTIVATION remains its
generic owner. The source clarification conserves the installed construction
basis's existing duties. The default, consuming Skills and index carry those
duties; the guide remains nondeciding. No other Product or installation is
migrated by this local selection.

===== END SOURCE =====

Source SHA-256: a843365899e6cf8ea75b6bbd2841b15b1bd6ef015f32228eb5197fdee06f5a80

[Original lines 1-95]
# Purpose index consumption

Skills must consume the independently owned Purpose Context runtime, catalog and
canonical instructions through an exact public manifest. Both hosts use one
adapter. Skills must not fork the compiler, rule semantics or catalog.

At relevant work, build, review, continuation or refresh points, resolve the
selected Product skill and catalog. Select an existing index only for its declared
source, method and purpose. Bind actual source bytes and supplied task facts;
then consume and check the resulting context through the Product public API.
No matching index is a scoped acquisition/authoring decision, not permission to
reuse a different family or silently weaken the purpose. Keep scope and grants
as caller facts, never inferred tool authority.

For release-targeted index work, resolve the latest owner-selected releasable
method and its exact manifest rather than using the constructor's older basis
as the corpus default. Consumer reuse still requires its actual selected method
to match. An unchanged purpose ID across release entries does not establish that
match. Preserve historical entries for older consumers; final-cut packaging must
refresh source/native bindings and obtain an applicable assessment with justified
evidence reuse, without inventing or relabeling a release.

True, false and unknown applicability remain distinct. Missing facts remain
unresolved; source drift and purpose mismatch withhold reuse. Retain actual
context bytes, selection identity and freshness result. A mechanically valid
index or context with no accepted semantic assessment remains trial-only and
must say so. The owning source remains available for required re-entry.

Independent review derives obligations from source before exposure to the
candidate-produced index/result when the deciding review contract requires it.
Neither this skill nor an index may certify its own source completeness.

The current successor purpose separates what a request authorizes from what a
future candidate actually preserves. Bind those facts to the caller's evidence,
not to desired coverage. A supported assessment for governing method constraints
leaves its retained construction, typing, testing, admission and acceptance
obligations to be performed. Forward the exact external assessment to both
consumption and check; no Skills-authored supported judgment or coverage flag is
created. The owning Product's canonical purpose definitions decide applicability.

## Default SDLC coverage

At a governed work, QA, finding-triage, repair, acceptance/release or drift-refresh
decision, select the Purpose Context family's declared SDLC workflow purpose and
bind the actual decision facts. A code-refactoring purpose cannot substitute for
that family. Missing coverage routes to the selected owning source and remains
explicit; the default flow must remain usable without authoring a new local law.

Keep ordinary incremental QA and named boundary assessment distinct. Preserve
the outcome, scope, exact subject and governing basis when presenting findings.
Reviewer evidence informs the decision owner; severity alone cannot assign
priority, expand work, hold unrelated landing or authorize another review round.
When failure recurs, use causal triage and the correct owning re-entry point.

Repairs retain failing-before/passing-after evidence and applicable suite results.
Use tests to verify a repair where the basis permits; an independent recheck
needs its actual trigger and covers affected obligations. Retain applicable
evidence for unchanged claims. Unknown evidence withholds the dependent claim;
it does not justify either a pass or an unrelated global work stop.

A frozen development source is a distinct explicit context selection with its
own identity, provenance and applicable assessment. It does not change the
installed native method, acquire a release identity or migrate a consumer.
The adapter forwards that selection unchanged through the same public API.
When source, purpose or defaults change, require the owner's rebuild/assessment
disposition before using an affected index; retain demonstrably unaffected work.

Acceptance uses the finite SDLC scenarios in SCENARIOS.md through the actual
Skills adapter and selected default instructions. Index/schema checks alone
cannot establish drift control, review termination or consumer compliance.

## Constitutional work-state and context continuity

The default workflow context must retain the existing TICKETS work-record,
carrier-to-execution and state-authority duties and ACTIVATION context,
restart and reuse duties. These come from the selected constitution. Default
frames, skills and guides project them and own no replacement law.

Bind the current ticket or other sufficient admitted carrier before selecting
task facts. Recover the admitted scope, source/evidence, applicable decisions,
residuals and next authorized action from that record and the actual workspace.
Keep work-state authority distinct from governing law and material evidence.
A summary or prior passing observation cannot overrule contrary current inputs.

Construct context for the actual next decision, using qualified accessible
references and source re-entry. Apply any declared acquisition capacity to its
stated boundary before loading, preserving unmeasured host inputs and unresolved
fit. At the permitted result boundary, retain step-produced facts and continuation
state through the existing actor/result and authorized work-state Writer routes.
Resumption checks invalidators and reconciles uncertain effects before retry;
it neither requires the old conversation nor resets retained valid work.

Use Q15-Q18 to observe this through the actual consuming path. Changed owning
source/default projections require affected index generation, exact assessment
and consumer-pin refresh; earlier corpus/query verdicts are not relabeled.

===== END SOURCE =====

# STDO Skills requirements

| ID | Required behavior |
|---|---|
| SK-01 | Both host bindings expose the exact selected native core workflows and preserve their relative references. Irrelevant work is not forced through an STDO pipeline. |
| SK-02 | Resolve one selected Product/method and verify the exact install; missing, ambiguous or changed identities withhold dependent operations. |
| SK-03 | Preserve existing grants, direct Writer sufficiency, native work ownership and source-defined coordination/review boundaries. |
| SK-04 | Invoke real supported public operations; retain failures and partial effects without fabricated success. |
| SK-05 | Use the independent Purpose Context Product for relevant task constraints; prefer a matching existing purpose index before scoped new authoring. |
| SK-06 | Preserve exact purpose, task facts, source/method identity and unknown applicability; stale source and purpose mismatch refuse index reuse. |
| SK-07 | Keep source-first independent review independent of candidate-derived indexes and answers. |
| SK-08 | Re-entry reacquires actual state and evidence; prior passing observations do not survive material drift automatically. |
| SK-09 | Report code/transport/semantic coverage/authority and performed/unperformed checks separately. |
| SK-10 | Install an exactly inventoried development package with independently pinned components; do not call it an immutable release. |
| SK-11 | Verify/remove only exact receipt-owned roots; preserve unrelated files and other installs. |
| SK-12 | Keep actual automatic host pickup, explicit invocation and local command execution as separate claims. |
| SK-14 | When explicitly selected, provide visual project/code documentation through the visual Product; no Lean or formal/context prerequisite. |
| SK-15 | Supply the adopted SDLC default at relevant work, QA, triage, repair and boundary decisions through the independently owned purpose index and original-source routes. |
| SK-16 | Preserve outcome, basis, scope and decision boundaries across review and refresh; material drift receives causal triage and lawful re-entry. |
| SK-17 | Support evidence-based repair termination and affected-claim rechecks while preserving required independence, acceptance gates and reusable valid evidence. |
| SK-18 | Demonstrate these behaviours through actual bounded consumer scenarios; distinguish explicit development-source selection, installed workflow, host pickup and release qualification. |
| SK-19 | Carry constitutional ticket/work-state authority and bounded context/restart duties through the native Skills and shared Purpose Context route. Bind the current sufficient carrier, actual source/evidence, qualified references and declared acquisition envelope; persist required results through the granted route. A guide, summary, index or new actor cannot replace that state, create a write grant or reset completed work. |
| SK-20 | Demonstrate continuation without inherited conversation, contrary stale-account correction, affected-evidence reuse, uncertain-effect restraint and capacity-bounded acquisition. Preserve lawful no-new-ticket work and role-specific result updates; do not infer behavior from installed words alone. |

The old registry-based SK-13 and formal support composition were withdrawn by the
owner's scope change. Their bytes/evidence remain historical, not satisfied by
renaming them. [Purpose index consumption](PURPOSE-INDEX-CONSUMPTION.md) defines
the current cross-Product use; [scenarios](SCENARIOS.md) define finite evidence.

Source SHA-256: 8b78121f3bc78bbb39336778b8d87b0f33d7e2f334838384301ec1977335da12

[Original lines 1-38]
# Finite scenarios and claim boundaries

Freeze exact package/dependencies, nominated Product and method, purpose/query,
source and destination before each observation. Record actual operations, output,
return code, effects and refused/unperformed checks. Local command tests do not
establish automatic host pickup. Source-first agent checks and native host tests
are distinct treatments; historical scenarios are not rerun by source proximity.

| Case | Required observation |
|---|---|
| Q01 Context-only fresh install | Codex and Claude workspace installs verify without a visual component or Lean. Native core frontmatter/references remain bound to the selected installed method. |
| Q02 Existing index use | Installed adapter resolves the independent Product, binds actual selected sources, selects a matching existing index, consumes context and checks it without new index authoring. |
| Q03 Source/purpose/unknown controls | Changed source and wrong purpose refuse reuse; missing deciding fact remains unknown/incomplete rather than silently true or covered. |
| Q04 Ownership and update boundary | Exact receipt verifies/removes owned roots only; altered owned bytes/broadened roots refuse; unrelated files survive. Existing historical installs are not silently migrated. |
| Q05 Optional visual | Explicit visual selection exposes the actual public visual path without Lean; no visual selection leaves context fully usable. |
| Q06 Host pickup | Separately observe automatic discovery and explicit skill invocation in actual Codex and Claude host sessions; command execution alone leaves this unestablished. |
| Q07 Native workflow | Actual intake, direct Writer, coordinated handoff/review, continuation after drift, interrupted effects and truthful status follow the native workflow and current authority. Wrapper inspection alone leaves behavioral qualification open. |
| Q08 Semantic value | Source-first independent assessment checks real deciding obligations, omissions and false additions on a declared workload; schema/identity checks do not establish general coverage or value. |
| Q09 Ordinary QA and repair | An authorized bounded candidate may land while selected QA runs; a reproduced repair closes by applicable evidence without an invented full review or release gate. |
| Q10 Recurring failure and drift | Evidence of a governing requirement/design/model/observation problem reaches its actual owner; changed outcome, scope, basis or assumptions are triaged before dependent action. Recurrence alone proves no particular cause. |
| Q11 Real acceptance boundary | A runnable Product/user-outcome claim retains its required acceptance evidence; local tests or a closed QA item cannot silently satisfy that boundary. |
| Q12 Unrelated findings | Preserve an out-of-claim observation and route it to its owner without automatically expanding or reopening the current claim; an actual shared dependency can invalidate affected reliance. |
| Q13 Incomplete evidence | Unknown material applicability or missing evidence leaves the dependent result unresolved and preserves unrelated authorized progress; no invented pass or blanket stop. |
| Q14 Reuse and stopping | Unchanged valid obligations retain their evidence. Only materially affected claims are reopened and independent rechecks have selected triggers; role changes and later findings alone do not restart full review. |
| Q15 Ticket-led continuation | A recipient without the prior conversation reconstructs the next authorized step from the current ticket/contract, actual workspace and linked evidence; it retains valid completed work and records its exact result through the permitted route. |
| Q16 Contrary retained account | A stale summary or ticket claim cannot overrule governing source or contrary current evidence. Reconcile through the authorized owner, reopen affected support and preserve unaffected work instead of restarting everything. |
| Q17 Unknown effect on resumption | Reacquire authorized effect observations before retry/dependent work. An inconclusive observation retains uncertainty and the required next owner/action; missing private context supplies no absent-effect proof. |
| Q18 Bounded context and sufficient carrier | For an admitted one-run task, use the permitted run carrier without inventing a durable ticket. Where a context capacity is declared, measure its selected acquisition boundary before reading, retain qualified references and explicit unmeasured host inputs, and refuse or lawfully refine overflow instead of clipping. |

Q09-Q18 use realistic inputs with expectations derived independently from owning
source before candidate context acquisition. Retain each actual request, selected
instructions/context identity, consumer response and assessment. Test related
cases in one bounded batch and reuse applicable support. A result applies only
to the observed actor, inputs, source and consumer path; it is not proof of general
effectiveness or automatic host triggering.

No single passing total hides an unperformed case. Report finite cases and limits,
not general reliability, complete corpus coverage or successor release readiness.

===== END SOURCE =====

# Representation Tools source bootstrap

Current purpose: source-linked visual documentation and exploration for a selected project. Select `stdo_a_c.json`, then read
[Intent](specification/INTENT.md), [Product](specification/PRODUCT.md),
[Goals](specification/GOALS.md) and [requirements](specification/requirements/README.md).
The stable Product identity and exact installed STDO 3.0.4 construction basis remain.

Purpose Context owns source-to-purpose context indexes. STDO Skills owns the
`stdo-context` adapter. This Product does not own agent context construction,
registry authority, workflow orchestration or proof execution. Keep visual source
binding, declared model structure and navigation; never infer semantic truth or
code correctness from a structural check or a diagram.

The November 12 owner direction authorizes retiring active legacy paths with
exact archives. Prior releases, installed copies, generated historical artifacts
and closed evidence retain their exact subjects. Current development source is
not the published 3.0.5-rc.1 package and is not a new qualified release.
See [alignment record](.ai-workspace/work/PURPOSE_CONTEXT_ALIGNMENT_2026-11-12/ACTIVATION.json).

<!-- STDO_BOOTSTRAP_START -->
## STDO Bootstrap

This scope is routed by an STDO Product Definition Overlay.

Before constitutional work:

1. Resolve the applicable `stdo_<label>.json` for the requested Product scope.
2. Use `constitution.stdo.basis`, not its mutable selector, as the operative basis.
3. Resolve and verify that exact installed release through the STDO toolchain manager.
4. Load the Product Definition's declared bootstrap entrypoint, then exact owning standards as needed.
5. Resolve the applicable accepted Project Reference-Frame Basis or its declared composition.
6. Bind the exact outcome, basis and existing work grant. Use direct Writer entry when the accepted frame configuration permits it and one capable context suffices; use Executive or the declared project equivalent for material coordination. Activate only applicable dependency-ready evaluations, preserving unknown applicability and required independence. Executive does not mutate files or Git; a separate Writer activation binds those effects.
7. Fail closed when the Product Definition, frame basis, subject, authority, or activation is missing, ambiguous, stale, or outside the governed scope.

Mutable methodology source, another installed version, a cache entry, and this
bootstrap cannot replace the exact basis selected by the Product Definition.
A prompt, summary, symbolic map, or prior result may route attention but cannot
replace current source authority or a closed frame result.
<!-- STDO_BOOTSTRAP_END -->

===== END SOURCE =====

# Representation Tools

Create and explore source-linked visual documentation. The stable Product lives
in `a_c/` and keeps its existing model/carrier formats for visual artifacts.

```sh
python3 -B scripts/ac.py --help
python3 -B scripts/ac.py project --help
python3 -B scripts/ac.py code --help
python3 -B scripts/ac.py explore --help
```

`project` binds a Product Definition and exact source, exposes authoring tasks,
checks submitted records, then builds a model, reversible carrier and offline
explorer. `code` adds selected code/test-source observations and attributed links;
it does not execute subject code or tests. `explore` reads an existing model.
`graph` supplies model/carrier checks. Optional `upstream` extraction and explicit
`bootstrap graphify` setup retain their own third-party pins.

The [development runtime manifest](PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json) declares portable visual
members. Direct source authoring/viewing requires no Lean, context registry or
workflow support runtime. Source fidelity remains an authored/reviewed property.

[Product](specification/PRODUCT.md) · [requirements](specification/requirements/README.md)
· [design](build_tenants/graphify/design/README.md)

Legacy nonvisual code and requirements are archived under [.ai-workspace/work/PURPOSE_CONTEXT_ALIGNMENT_2026-11-12](.ai-workspace/work/PURPOSE_CONTEXT_ALIGNMENT_2026-11-12/ACTIVATION.json).
Local installed Lean/toolchain caches remain outside current Product membership.
An over-broad archive pass was corrected by exact restoration; see the
[restoration readback](.ai-workspace/work/PURPOSE_CONTEXT_ALIGNMENT_2026-11-12/INSTALLED-RESTORATION-READBACK.json).
Published 3.0.5-rc.1 and prior evidence remain historical exact subjects; this
changed development source has no new package or release qualification.

Help a project reader understand exact source through visual records, relations,
constraints, conditions, exceptions, unresolved meaning and source navigation.

offline exploration and additive code/test-source mapping. It withdraws
nonvisual context construction, registry-as-authority, F_D orchestration and

unsatisfied, not completed by the visual implementation. Purpose Context owns
context indexing; STDO Skills owns its host adapter.

from a valid model. Prior work, releases and evidence preserve their own scope.

Source SHA-256: 78b27b2525c99f8dbb273aba646282e0b67d52175961d2681dee54b6ec36c8a2

[Original lines 1-36]
# Representation Tools Product

## Product boundary

Stable definition `urn:stdo:product-definition:a-c-implementations` owns generic
source-linked visual documentation. The retained Graphify tenant implements
source generation, declared model/carrier preservation, offline exploration,
source navigation and additive code/test-source overlays. STDO Representation
owns its STDO-specific source interpretation; Axiom Indexer owns its separately
selected mechanical conversion/projection. Purpose Context owns purpose indexes.

## Public behavior

The public router is `scripts/ac.py`: `project`, `code`, `explore`, `graph`,
optional `upstream`, and explicit isolated `bootstrap graphify`. A project build
binds its definition, source inventory and hashes, author submissions and
machinery. Reuse refuses changed inputs. A model check verifies declared structure
and references; it does not discover missing meaning or accept source fidelity.
The visual overlay preserves the base and distinguishes observations from
attributed mappings, unknown endpoints and unexecuted test-source links.

## Current source and history

Active runtime no longer imports Lean/formal, support, registry or task-context
implementations. Prior proof-selected runs require their exact archived runtime;
they are not silently reinterpreted as current visual runs. Retired code, tests,
requirements and original dirty source are preserved in the alignment archive.
Existing generated artifacts, installed caches and immutable releases remain.
There is no new release/installation claim or automatic migration of old users.

## Product disposition authority

The owner directs this scope and retirement; Root binds scoped Writer effects.
The current exact construction basis remains STDO 3.0.4. Earlier T-020 C1–C7
context obligations are withdrawn unsatisfied. The completed comparison remains
evidence for the separate context direction, not a visual capability acceptance.

Source SHA-256: 096c4c0a6f5cbd380f860e78dd031614674a2b9ab4bf176f9fd48b02e52bcff2

[Original lines 1-66]
# Code and realization traces

This capability connects an exact source representation to selected project
code and tests. It refines the [visual requirements](README.md) and [project use](project-use.md). Acquisition remains confined to explicit local source selection; no lifecycle, context delivery or proof capability is introduced.

## Required outcomes

**CR-01 — Exact selection and lawful acquisition.** Bind the nominated Product,
exact base representation and its source basis, and explicitly selected local
code/test files with their content identities. Directory selection records its
member set. Acquire only within the selected scope and existing permissions;
an import, graph edge, nearby folder or common repository creates no implicit
route into another Product. Unsupported acquisition routes refuse.

**CR-02 — Additive representation.** Supply a versioned code-and-trace overlay
bound to that exact base. Preserve the existing native model and carrier bytes,
record identities and declared dependency semantics. An unknown overlay version
or inconsistent base binding refuses. Existing validation and historical evidence
retain their original subject and proof scope; adding an overlay does not extend
them to code or trace assertions.

**CR-03 — Distinct observations and interpretations.** Identify extracted
structural observations separately from caller-attributed F_P mappings such as
`implements`, `supports` and `tests`. Preserve the extractor's supported scope
and each mapping's declared author, origin and meaning. Unknown or ambiguous
endpoints, unsupported files and failed extraction remain inspectable; they
cannot be silently resolved by matching names or presented as an empty successful
extraction. A discovered call/import is not a normative dependency. No structural
observation or trace link establishes implementation correctness, requirement
satisfaction, test execution or result, semantic admission or formal proof.

**CR-04 — Recoverable identities and provenance.** Preserve record and directed
relation identities, distinct parallel/self-relations and repeated occurrences.
Bind code observations and mappings to exact source content, available source
coordinates and producing inputs/tools. Retain unresolved endpoint descriptions
and alternatives without substituting an arbitrary resolved target. Readers can
distinguish observed source, caller assertions and unavailable evidence.

**CR-05 — Readable trace navigation.** The same portable offline reader can
follow a selected requirement to its mapped code and tests, inspect the linked
source and return through the reading trail. Its views distinguish source-model
relations, code observations and F_P mappings, preserving selection when changing
renderers or moving between Files and logical views. Display missing or unresolved
links and extraction coverage honestly. No mapped test means only that no such
link is available in this selected representation, not that the project has no
test. Reading the export needs neither a service nor the extractor installation.

**CR-06 — Currentness and reuse.** Bind reuse to the base model/source, selected
code/test bytes and membership, mapping assertions, overlay profile and relevant
extraction/rendering machinery and options. A material change invalidates the
affected reusable result; report its scope and retain prior evidence as history.
Rechecking identity does not renew a semantic judgment or grant new access.

**CR-07 — Public-path evidence.** Provide documented public construction and
inspection operations using the existing project workflow. Independently assess
the actual supported extractor at an exact pinned version and the generated
reader, including source-to-observation samples and requirement→code→test
navigation. Exercise unresolved/ambiguous endpoints, unsupported/failed extraction,
parallel relations, base preservation and stale-input controls. Report checked
properties, coverage and remaining uncertainty. Semantic fidelity, test outcomes,
whole-project qualification and any quality or cost benefit require their own
evidence under the existing AC-R and PU contracts.

[T-019](../../.ai-workspace/tickets/active/T-019-code-representation.md) selects
the first public CLI/reader slice. Concrete languages, extractor, schema, commands
and storage belong to tenant HOW; this common contract does not mandate Graphify.

===== END SOURCE =====

# Visual project use

|---|---|
| PU-01 | Bind the caller's Product Definition, exact method/source cut and selected local source; refuse escaping or redirected source. |
| PU-02 | Accept source-grounded authored records with coverage dispositions, assumptions and residuals; discovered code edges do not become normative relations. |
| PU-03 | Produce portable full-record documentation with constraints, conditions, exceptions, source links and 2D/3D navigation; offline reading needs no authoring stack. |
| PU-04 | Report structural validation with its exact subject and limits; no source completeness or proof claim. |
| PU-06 | Refuse stale reuse and preserve previous models and evidence. |
| PU-08 | Supply documented portable visual entrypoints and explicit optional extraction dependencies. |
| PU-09 | Assess the actual non-STDO visual path and source fidelity before integrated qualification claims. |

PU-05 agent-context delivery and PU-07 workflow/recipient composition are
withdrawn from this Product, unsatisfied where previously open. Their original
text remains archived. A public visual path does not grant mutation, access,
semantic acceptance or new release standing.
